A grown-up-sized long-sleeved belted romper in corduroy, with fitted knee-length legs, sewn entirely from real fabric as a wearable version of the outfit of a carnival barker. On the upper body it is a striped waistcoat and a boater, sewn from chenille and plush towelling, with belts, patches and buttons attached as real pieces. trousers forms the bottom half in polar fleece, stitched seamlessly into the top. An attached hood at the neck is sewn as a straw boater in chenille. A fabric version of megaphone is stitched onto the suit. Every part is real fabric, panels cut, stitched together and finished with seam binding, with the outfit's details made as sewn pieces. The outfit is laid out on the garment's own rectangle shape, fully covering it from side seam to side seam. It is a complete costume of carnival barker in one piece, shown full length from collar to ankles, with a striped waistcoat and a boater, trousers on the legs, a straw boater (as the hood), shoes (as the feet), a megaphone sewn or attached to the suit. The upper half carries the top and the leg tubes carry the lower garment, so the whole outfit reads at once. Novelty embellishments, fluffy sherpa patches at the elbows and knees, knitted rib panels with raised cable texture and puffy padded shoulder pieces. The embellishments are sewn on as real raised fabric, so the suit has depth, texture and sparkle from every angle.

The top half is built as a draped robe with snake hair, cut from polar fleece and velvet in the outfit's colours, with a real sewn collar, real pockets, buttons or fastenings, and trims stitched on. An attached hood at the neck is sewn as a snake crown in polar fleece. The garment is assembled from cut panels of fabric joined with visible seams, topstitching and piping, and it hangs as one coherent piece with every part in the outfit's own cloth. The uniform fills the whole straight-sided garment shape edge to edge, the same width at chest, waist and hem. Nothing of the outfit is missing and the legs of the suit show the lower garment. A slightly oversized adult-sized long-sleeved halter-neck romper in polar fleece, with fitted knee-length legs, sewn entirely from real fabric as a wearable version of the outfit of an armoured mercenary. An attached hood at the neck is sewn as a full helmet in minky plush. A fabric version of rifle is stitched onto the suit. Every part is real fabric, panels cut, stitched together and finished with seam binding, with the outfit's details made as sewn pieces. The outfit is stretched to the garment's own straight-sided shape and fills it completely, so it suits any body size.
